+                                                    std::min(N_, usefulDevices));
+        devices_.reserve(deviceCount);
+
+        const size_t baseRows = N_ / deviceCount;
+        const size_t remainder = N_ % deviceCount;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+
+        try {
+            for (size_t index = 0; index < deviceCount; ++index) {
+                DeviceContext context;
+                context.device = static_cast<int>(index);
+                context.firstRow = firstRow;
+                context.rows = baseRows + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+                devices_.push_back(context);
+
+                DeviceContext& device = devices_.back();
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&device.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                          "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+                checkCublas(cublasCreate(&device.handle), "cublasCreate");
+                checkCublas(cublasSetStream(device.handle, device.stream), "cublasSetStream");
+
+                const size_t sliceElements = device.rows * N_;
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.A),
+                                     sliceElements * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(A slice)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.B),
+                                     N_ * N_ * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(B)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.C),
+                                     sliceElements * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(C slice)");
+
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.A, A.data() + firstRow * N_,
+                                          sliceElements * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync(A slice)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.B, B.data(), N_ * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync(B)");
+
+                firstRow += device.rows;
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            synchronize();
+
+            // Warm up CUDA context/module loading so it is not charged to the
+            // benchmark. The measured GEMM completely overwrites this result.
+            launchGemm();
+            synchronize();
+        } catch (...) {
+            release();
+            throw;
         }
     }
+
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier(const CudaMatrixMultiplier&) = delete;
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier& operator=(const CudaMatrixMultiplier&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaMatrixMultiplier() {
+        release();
+    }
+
+    double multiply() {
+        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+        launchGemm();
+        synchronize();
+        const auto end = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+        return std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(end - start).count();
+    }
+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * N_, device.C,
+                                      device.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, device.stream),
+                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C slice)");
+        }
+        synchronize();
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceCount() const noexcept {
+        return devices_.size();
+    }
+
+private:
+    struct DeviceContext {
+        int device = 0;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+        size_t rows = 0;
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+        double* A = nullptr;
+        double* B = nullptr;
+        double* C = nullptr;
+    };
+
+    void launchGemm() {
+        constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+        constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+
+            // cuBLAS uses column-major storage. A row-major C = A * B is the
+            // same memory as column-major C^T = B^T * A^T, hence the swapped
+            // operand order below. A partition becomes N-by-rows in this view.
+            checkCublas(cublasDgemm(device.handle,
+                                    CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                    dimension_, static_cast<int>(device.rows), dimension_,
+                                    &alpha,
+                                    device.B, dimension_,
+                                    device.A, dimension_,
+                                    &beta,
+                                    device.C, dimension_),
+                        "cublasDgemm");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() noexcept {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            cudaSetDevice(device.device);
+            if (device.handle != nullptr) {
+                cublasDestroy(device.handle);
+            }
+            if (device.A != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.A);
+            }
+            if (device.B != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.B);
+            }
+            if (device.C != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.C);
+            }
+            if (device.stream != nullptr) {
+                cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
+            }
+        }
+        devices_.clear();
+    }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    int dimension_;
+    std::vector<DeviceContext> devices_;
+};
+
+size_t parseMatrixSize(const char* text) {
+    if (text[0] == '\0' || text[0] == '-') {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (*end != '\0' || value == 0 || value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+    if (value > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size exceeds the cuBLAS dimension limit");
+    }
+
+    const size_t N = static_cast<size_t>(value);
+    if (N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / N ||
+        N * N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size is too large");
+    }
+    return N;
 }

+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }

+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cublasStatusString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each GPU owns a disjoint group of output rows. B is replicated, while A and
+// C are partitioned, so the GEMMs can execute independently and concurrently.
+class CudaMatrixMultiplier {
+public:
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier(const std::vector<double>& A,
+                         const std::vector<double>& B,
+                         size_t N)
+        : N_(N), dimension_(static_cast<int>(N)) {
+        int availableDevices = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&availableDevices), "cudaGetDeviceCount");
+        if (availableDevices == 0) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("no CUDA-capable GPU is available");
+        }
+
+        // A slice of at least 256 rows gives cuBLAS enough work to amortize a
+        // launch on current GPUs while still scaling larger matrices broadly.
+        constexpr size_t targetRowsPerDevice = 256;
+        const size_t usefulDevices = std::max<size_t>(1, N_ / targetRowsPerDevice);
+        const size_t deviceCount = std::min<size_t>(availableDevices,
+                                                    std::min(N_, usefulDevices));
+        devices_.reserve(deviceCount);
+
+        const size_t baseRows = N_ / deviceCount;
+        const size_t remainder = N_ % deviceCount;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+
+        try {
+            for (size_t index = 0; index < deviceCount; ++index) {
+                DeviceContext context;
+                context.device = static_cast<int>(index);
+                context.firstRow = firstRow;
+                context.rows = baseRows + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+                devices_.push_back(context);
+
+                DeviceContext& device = devices_.back();
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&device.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                          "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+                checkCublas(cublasCreate(&device.handle), "cublasCreate");
+                checkCublas(cublasSetStream(device.handle, device.stream), "cublasSetStream");
+
+                const size_t sliceElements = device.rows * N_;
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.A),
+                                     sliceElements * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(A slice)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.B),
+                                     N_ * N_ * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(B)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.C),
+                                     sliceElements * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(C slice)");
+
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.A, A.data() + firstRow * N_,
+                                          sliceElements * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync(A slice)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.B, B.data(), N_ * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync(B)");
+
+                firstRow += device.rows;
+            }
+            synchronize();
+
+            // Warm up CUDA context/module loading so it is not charged to the
+            // benchmark. The measured GEMM completely overwrites this result.
+            launchGemm();
+            synchronize();
+        } catch (...) {
+            release();
+            throw;
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier(const CudaMatrixMultiplier&) = delete;
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier& operator=(const CudaMatrixMultiplier&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaMatrixMultiplier() {
+        release();
+    }
+
+    double multiply() {
+        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+        launchGemm();
+        synchronize();
+        const auto end = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+        return std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(end - start).count();
+    }
+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * N_, device.C,
+                                      device.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, device.stream),
+                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C slice)");
+        }
+        synchronize();
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceCount() const noexcept {
+        return devices_.size();
+    }
+
+private:
+    struct DeviceContext {
+        int device = 0;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+        size_t rows = 0;
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+        double* A = nullptr;
+        double* B = nullptr;
+        double* C = nullptr;
+    };
+
+    void launchGemm() {
+        constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+        constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+
+            // cuBLAS uses column-major storage. A row-major C = A * B is the
+            // same memory as column-major C^T = B^T * A^T, hence the swapped
+            // operand order below. A partition becomes N-by-rows in this view.
+            checkCublas(cublasDgemm(device.handle,
+                                    CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                    dimension_, static_cast<int>(device.rows), dimension_,
+                                    &alpha,
+                                    device.B, dimension_,
+                                    device.A, dimension_,
+                                    &beta,
+                                    device.C, dimension_),
+                        "cublasDgemm");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() noexcept {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            cudaSetDevice(device.device);
+            if (device.handle != nullptr) {
+                cublasDestroy(device.handle);
+            }
+            if (device.A != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.A);
+            }
+            if (device.B != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.B);
+            }
+            if (device.C != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.C);
             }

+            if (device.stream != nullptr) {
+                cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
+            }
         }
+        devices_.clear();
     }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    int dimension_;
+    std::vector<DeviceContext> devices_;
+};

+size_t parseMatrixSize(const char* text) {
+    if (text[0] == '\0' || text[0] == '-') {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (*end != '\0' || value == 0 || value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+    if (value > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size exceeds the cuBLAS dimension limit");
+    }
+
+    const size_t N = static_cast<size_t>(value);
+    if (N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / N ||
+        N * N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size is too large");
+    }
+    return N;
 }

+        std::vector<double> A(N * N);
+        std::vector<double> B(N * N);
+        std::vector<double> C(N * N);
+
+        // Initialize matrices
+        printf("Initializing matrices...\n");
+        initMatrix(A, N);
+        initMatrix(B, N);
+
+        CudaMatrixMultiplier multiplier(A, B, N);
+        printf("CUDA devices used: %zu\n", multiplier.deviceCount());
+
+        // Perform matrix multiplication
+        printf("Computing matrix multiplication...\n");
+        const double durationMs = multiplier.multiply();
+        multiplier.copyResult(C);
+
+        printf("Computation time: %.3f ms\n", durationMs);
+
+        // Calculate GFLOPS without overflowing an integer intermediate.
+        const double dimension = static_cast<double>(N);
+        const double gflops = (2.0 * dimension * dimension * dimension) /
+                              (durationMs * 1.0e6);
+        printf("Performance: %.3f GFLOPS\n", gflops);
+
+        // Print results for external validation
+        if (printResults) {
+            print_results(C, "MatrixC");
+        }
+
+        // Validation
+        if (validate) {
+            printf("Validating result...\n");
+            const bool valid = validateResult(A, B, C, N);
+
+            if (valid) {
+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+                return 0;
+            }
             printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
             return 1;
         }
+    } catch (const std::exception& error) {
+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA matrix multiplication failed: %s\n", error.what());
+        return 1;
     }
-    
+
     return 0;
 }

+const char* cublasStatusString(cublasStatus_t status) noexcept {
+    switch (status) {
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS: return "success";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_INITIALIZED: return "not initialized";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ALLOC_FAILED: return "allocation failed";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_INVALID_VALUE: return "invalid value";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ARCH_MISMATCH: return "architecture mismatch";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_MAPPING_ERROR: return "mapping error";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_EXECUTION_FAILED: return "execution failed";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_INTERNAL_ERROR: return "internal error";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED: return "not supported";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_LICENSE_ERROR: return "license error";
+        default: return "unknown error";
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cublasStatusString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each GPU owns a disjoint group of output rows. B is replicated, while A and
+// C are partitioned, so the GEMMs can execute independently and concurrently.
+class CudaMatrixMultiplier {
+public:
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier(const std::vector<double>& A,
+                         const std::vector<double>& B,
+                         size_t N)
+        : N_(N), dimension_(static_cast<int>(N)) {
+        int availableDevices = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&availableDevices), "cudaGetDeviceCount");
+        if (availableDevices == 0) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("no CUDA-capable GPU is available");
+        }
+
+        // A slice of at least 256 rows gives cuBLAS enough work to amortize a
+        // launch on current GPUs while still scaling larger matrices broadly.
+        constexpr size_t targetRowsPerDevice = 256;
+        const size_t usefulDevices = std::max<size_t>(1, N_ / targetRowsPerDevice);
+        const size_t deviceCount = std::min<size_t>(availableDevices,
+                                                    std::min(N_, usefulDevices));
+        devices_.reserve(deviceCount);
+
+        const size_t baseRows = N_ / deviceCount;
+        const size_t remainder = N_ % deviceCount;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+
+        try {
+            for (size_t index = 0; index < deviceCount; ++index) {
+                DeviceContext context;
+                context.device = static_cast<int>(index);
+                context.firstRow = firstRow;
+                context.rows = baseRows + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+                devices_.push_back(context);
+
+                DeviceContext& device = devices_.back();
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&device.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                          "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+                checkCublas(cublasCreate(&device.handle), "cublasCreate");
+                checkCublas(cublasSetStream(device.handle, device.stream), "cublasSetStream");
+
+                const size_t sliceElements = device.rows * N_;
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.A),
+                                     sliceElements * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(A slice)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.B),
+                                     N_ * N_ * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(B)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.C),
+                                     sliceElements * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(C slice)");
+
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.A, A.data() + firstRow * N_,
+                                          sliceElements * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync(A slice)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.B, B.data(), N_ * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync(B)");
+
+                firstRow += device.rows;
+            }
+            synchronize();
+
+            // Warm up CUDA context/module loading so it is not charged to the
+            // benchmark. The measured GEMM completely overwrites this result.
+            launchGemm();
+            synchronize();
+        } catch (...) {
+            release();
+            throw;
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier(const CudaMatrixMultiplier&) = delete;
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier& operator=(const CudaMatrixMultiplier&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaMatrixMultiplier() {
+        release();
+    }
+
+    double multiply() {
+        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+        launchGemm();
+        synchronize();
+        const auto end = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+        return std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(end - start).count();
+    }
+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * N_, device.C,
+                                      device.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, device.stream),
+                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C slice)");
+        }
+        synchronize();
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceCount() const noexcept {
+        return devices_.size();
+    }
+
+private:
+    struct DeviceContext {
+        int device = 0;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+        size_t rows = 0;
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+        double* A = nullptr;
+        double* B = nullptr;
+        double* C = nullptr;
+    };
+
+    void launchGemm() {
+        constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+        constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+
+            // cuBLAS uses column-major storage. A row-major C = A * B is the
+            // same memory as column-major C^T = B^T * A^T, hence the swapped
+            // operand order below. A partition becomes N-by-rows in this view.
+            checkCublas(cublasDgemm(device.handle,
+                                    CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                    dimension_, static_cast<int>(device.rows), dimension_,
+                                    &alpha,
+                                    device.B, dimension_,
+                                    device.A, dimension_,
+                                    &beta,
+                                    device.C, dimension_),
+                        "cublasDgemm");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() noexcept {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            cudaSetDevice(device.device);
+            if (device.handle != nullptr) {
+                cublasDestroy(device.handle);
+            }
+            if (device.A != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.A);
+            }
+            if (device.B != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.B);
+            }
+            if (device.C != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.C);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            if (device.stream != nullptr) {
+                cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
+            }
         }
+        devices_.clear();
     }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    int dimension_;
+    std::vector<DeviceContext> devices_;
+};
+
+size_t parseMatrixSize(const char* text) {
+    if (text[0] == '\0' || text[0] == '-') {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (*end != '\0' || value == 0 || value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+    if (value > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size exceeds the cuBLAS dimension limit");
+    }
+
+    const size_t N = static_cast<size_t>(value);
+    if (N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / N ||
+        N * N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size is too large");
+    }
+    return N;
 }

+    double multiply() {
+        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+        launchGemm();
+        synchronize();
+        const auto end = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+        return std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(end - start).count();
+    }
+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * N_, device.C,
+                                      device.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, device.stream),
+                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C slice)");
+        }
+        synchronize();
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceCount() const noexcept {
+        return devices_.size();
+    }
+
+private:
+    struct DeviceContext {
+        int device = 0;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+        size_t rows = 0;
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+        double* A = nullptr;
+        double* B = nullptr;
+        double* C = nullptr;
+    };
+
+    void launchGemm() {
+        constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+        constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+
+            // cuBLAS uses column-major storage. A row-major C = A * B is the
+            // same memory as column-major C^T = B^T * A^T, hence the swapped
+            // operand order below. A partition becomes N-by-rows in this view.
+            checkCublas(cublasDgemm(device.handle,
+                                    CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                    dimension_, static_cast<int>(device.rows), dimension_,
+                                    &alpha,
+                                    device.B, dimension_,
+                                    device.A, dimension_,
+                                    &beta,
+                                    device.C, dimension_),
+                        "cublasDgemm");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() noexcept {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            cudaSetDevice(device.device);
+            if (device.handle != nullptr) {
+                cublasDestroy(device.handle);
+            }
+            if (device.A != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.A);
+            }
+            if (device.B != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.B);
+            }
+            if (device.C != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.C);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            if (device.stream != nullptr) {
+                cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
+            }
         }
+        devices_.clear();
     }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    int dimension_;
+    std::vector<DeviceContext> devices_;
+};
+
+size_t parseMatrixSize(const char* text) {
+    if (text[0] == '\0' || text[0] == '-') {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (*end != '\0' || value == 0 || value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+    if (value > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size exceeds the cuBLAS dimension limit");
+    }
+
+    const size_t N = static_cast<size_t>(value);
+    if (N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / N ||
+        N * N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size is too large");
+    }
+    return N;
 }

codex
The first performance pass is clean: validation passed at uneven and even partitions from 5×5 through 4096×4096. On the four RTX 3090s, measured FP64 throughput reached about 1.91 TFLOP/s at 4096×4096 versus roughly 0.42 TFLOP/s on one GPU at 1024×1024. I’ve also strengthened validation to sample the middle and final rows/columns, which catches mistakes at GPU slice boundaries.

+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_INITIALIZED: return "not initialized";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ALLOC_FAILED: return "allocation failed";

+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ARCH_MISMATCH: return "architecture mismatch";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_MAPPING_ERROR: return "mapping error";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_EXECUTION_FAILED: return "execution failed";

+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_LICENSE_ERROR: return "license error";
+        default: return "unknown error";

+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cublasStatusString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each GPU owns a disjoint group of output rows. B is replicated, while A and
+// C are partitioned, so the GEMMs can execute independently and concurrently.
+class CudaMatrixMultiplier {
+public:
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier(const std::vector<double>& A,
+                         const std::vector<double>& B,
+                         size_t N)
+        : N_(N), dimension_(static_cast<int>(N)) {
+        int availableDevices = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&availableDevices), "cudaGetDeviceCount");
+        if (availableDevices == 0) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("no CUDA-capable GPU is available");
+        }
+
+        // A slice of at least 256 rows gives cuBLAS enough work to amortize a
+        // launch on current GPUs while still scaling larger matrices broadly.
+        constexpr size_t targetRowsPerDevice = 256;
+        const size_t usefulDevices = std::max<size_t>(1, N_ / targetRowsPerDevice);
+        const size_t deviceCount = std::min<size_t>(availableDevices,
+                                                    std::min(N_, usefulDevices));
+        devices_.reserve(deviceCount);
+
+        const size_t baseRows = N_ / deviceCount;
+        const size_t remainder = N_ % deviceCount;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+
+        try {
+            for (size_t index = 0; index < deviceCount; ++index) {
+                DeviceContext context;
+                context.device = static_cast<int>(index);
+                context.firstRow = firstRow;
+                context.rows = baseRows + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+                devices_.push_back(context);
+
+                DeviceContext& device = devices_.back();
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&device.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                          "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+                checkCublas(cublasCreate(&device.handle), "cublasCreate");
+                checkCublas(cublasSetStream(device.handle, device.stream), "cublasSetStream");
+
+                const size_t sliceElements = device.rows * N_;
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.A),
+                                     sliceElements * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(A slice)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.B),
+                                     N_ * N_ * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(B)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.C),
+                                     sliceElements * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(C slice)");
+
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.A, A.data() + firstRow * N_,
+                                          sliceElements * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync(A slice)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.B, B.data(), N_ * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync(B)");
+
+                firstRow += device.rows;
+            }
+            synchronize();
+
+            // Warm up CUDA context/module loading so it is not charged to the
+            // benchmark. The measured GEMM completely overwrites this result.
+            launchGemm();
+            synchronize();
+        } catch (...) {
+            release();
+            throw;
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier(const CudaMatrixMultiplier&) = delete;
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier& operator=(const CudaMatrixMultiplier&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaMatrixMultiplier() {
+        release();
+    }
+
+    double multiply() {
+        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+        launchGemm();
+        synchronize();
+        const auto end = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+        return std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(end - start).count();
+    }
+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * N_, device.C,
+                                      device.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, device.stream),
+                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C slice)");
+        }
+        synchronize();
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceCount() const noexcept {
+        return devices_.size();
+    }
+
+private:
+    struct DeviceContext {
+        int device = 0;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+        size_t rows = 0;
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+        double* A = nullptr;
+        double* B = nullptr;
+        double* C = nullptr;
+    };
+
+    void launchGemm() {
+        constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+        constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+
+            // cuBLAS uses column-major storage. A row-major C = A * B is the
+            // same memory as column-major C^T = B^T * A^T, hence the swapped
+            // operand order below. A partition becomes N-by-rows in this view.
+            checkCublas(cublasDgemm(device.handle,
+                                    CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                    dimension_, static_cast<int>(device.rows), dimension_,
+                                    &alpha,
+                                    device.B, dimension_,
+                                    device.A, dimension_,
+                                    &beta,
+                                    device.C, dimension_),
+                        "cublasDgemm");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() noexcept {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            cudaSetDevice(device.device);
+            if (device.handle != nullptr) {
+                cublasDestroy(device.handle);
+            }
+            if (device.A != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.A);
+            }
+            if (device.B != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.B);
+            }
+            if (device.C != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.C);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            if (device.stream != nullptr) {
+                cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
+            }
         }
+        devices_.clear();
     }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    int dimension_;
+    std::vector<DeviceContext> devices_;
+};

+size_t parseMatrixSize(const char* text) {
+    if (text[0] == '\0' || text[0] == '-') {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (*end != '\0' || value == 0 || value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+    if (value > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size exceeds the cuBLAS dimension limit");
+    }

+    return N;

+                printf("Validation: PASSED\n");
+                return 0;
+            }
             printf("Validation: FAILED\n");
             return 1;
         }

+        fprintf(stderr, "CUDA matrix multiplication failed: %s\n", error.what());
+        return 1;
     }

     return 0;

+const char* cublasStatusString(cublasStatus_t status) noexcept {
+    switch (status) {
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS: return "success";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_INITIALIZED: return "not initialized";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ALLOC_FAILED: return "allocation failed";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_INVALID_VALUE: return "invalid value";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ARCH_MISMATCH: return "architecture mismatch";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_MAPPING_ERROR: return "mapping error";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_EXECUTION_FAILED: return "execution failed";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_INTERNAL_ERROR: return "internal error";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED: return "not supported";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_LICENSE_ERROR: return "license error";
+        default: return "unknown error";
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cublasStatusString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each GPU owns a disjoint group of output rows. B is replicated, while A and
+// C are partitioned, so the GEMMs can execute independently and concurrently.
+class CudaMatrixMultiplier {
+public:
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier(const std::vector<double>& A,
+                         const std::vector<double>& B,
+                         size_t N)
+        : N_(N), dimension_(static_cast<int>(N)) {
+        int availableDevices = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&availableDevices), "cudaGetDeviceCount");
+        if (availableDevices == 0) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("no CUDA-capable GPU is available");
+        }
+
+        // A slice of at least 256 rows gives cuBLAS enough work to amortize a
+        // launch on current GPUs while still scaling larger matrices broadly.
+        constexpr size_t targetRowsPerDevice = 256;
+        const size_t usefulDevices = std::max<size_t>(1, N_ / targetRowsPerDevice);
+        const size_t deviceCount = std::min<size_t>(availableDevices,
+                                                    std::min(N_, usefulDevices));
+        devices_.reserve(deviceCount);
+
+        const size_t baseRows = N_ / deviceCount;
+        const size_t remainder = N_ % deviceCount;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+
+        try {
+            for (size_t index = 0; index < deviceCount; ++index) {
+                DeviceContext context;
+                context.device = static_cast<int>(index);
+                context.firstRow = firstRow;
+                context.rows = baseRows + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+                devices_.push_back(context);
+
+                DeviceContext& device = devices_.back();
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&device.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                          "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+                checkCublas(cublasCreate(&device.handle), "cublasCreate");
+                checkCublas(cublasSetStream(device.handle, device.stream), "cublasSetStream");
+
+                const size_t sliceElements = device.rows * N_;
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.A),
+                                     sliceElements * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(A slice)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.B),
+                                     N_ * N_ * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(B)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.C),
+                                     sliceElements * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(C slice)");
+
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.A, A.data() + firstRow * N_,
+                                          sliceElements * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync(A slice)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.B, B.data(), N_ * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync(B)");
+
+                firstRow += device.rows;
+            }
+            synchronize();
+
+            // Warm up CUDA context/module loading so it is not charged to the
+            // benchmark. The measured GEMM completely overwrites this result.
+            launchGemm();
+            synchronize();
+        } catch (...) {
+            release();
+            throw;
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier(const CudaMatrixMultiplier&) = delete;
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier& operator=(const CudaMatrixMultiplier&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaMatrixMultiplier() {
+        release();
+    }
+
+    double multiply() {
+        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+        launchGemm();
+        synchronize();
+        const auto end = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+        return std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(end - start).count();
+    }
+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * N_, device.C,
+                                      device.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, device.stream),
+                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C slice)");
+        }
+        synchronize();
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceCount() const noexcept {
+        return devices_.size();
+    }
+
+private:
+    struct DeviceContext {
+        int device = 0;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+        size_t rows = 0;
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+        double* A = nullptr;
+        double* B = nullptr;
+        double* C = nullptr;
+    };
+
+    void launchGemm() {
+        constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+        constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+
+            // cuBLAS uses column-major storage. A row-major C = A * B is the
+            // same memory as column-major C^T = B^T * A^T, hence the swapped
+            // operand order below. A partition becomes N-by-rows in this view.
+            checkCublas(cublasDgemm(device.handle,
+                                    CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                    dimension_, static_cast<int>(device.rows), dimension_,
+                                    &alpha,
+                                    device.B, dimension_,
+                                    device.A, dimension_,
+                                    &beta,
+                                    device.C, dimension_),
+                        "cublasDgemm");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() noexcept {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            cudaSetDevice(device.device);
+            if (device.handle != nullptr) {
+                cublasDestroy(device.handle);
+            }
+            if (device.A != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.A);
+            }
+            if (device.B != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.B);
+            }
+            if (device.C != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.C);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            if (device.stream != nullptr) {
+                cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
+            }
         }
+        devices_.clear();
     }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    int dimension_;
+    std::vector<DeviceContext> devices_;
+};
+
+size_t parseMatrixSize(const char* text) {
+    if (text[0] == '\0' || text[0] == '-') {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (*end != '\0' || value == 0 || value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+    if (value > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size exceeds the cuBLAS dimension limit");
+    }
+
+    const size_t N = static_cast<size_t>(value);
+    if (N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / N ||
+        N * N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size is too large");
+    }
+    return N;
 }

+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * N_, device.C,
+                                      device.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, device.stream),
+                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C slice)");
+        }
+        synchronize();
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceCount() const noexcept {
+        return devices_.size();
+    }
+
+private:
+    struct DeviceContext {
+        int device = 0;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+        size_t rows = 0;
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+        double* A = nullptr;
+        double* B = nullptr;
+        double* C = nullptr;
+    };
+
+    void launchGemm() {
+        constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+        constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+
+            // cuBLAS uses column-major storage. A row-major C = A * B is the
+            // same memory as column-major C^T = B^T * A^T, hence the swapped
+            // operand order below. A partition becomes N-by-rows in this view.
+            checkCublas(cublasDgemm(device.handle,
+                                    CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                    dimension_, static_cast<int>(device.rows), dimension_,
+                                    &alpha,
+                                    device.B, dimension_,
+                                    device.A, dimension_,
+                                    &beta,
+                                    device.C, dimension_),
+                        "cublasDgemm");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() noexcept {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            cudaSetDevice(device.device);
+            if (device.handle != nullptr) {
+                cublasDestroy(device.handle);
+            }
+            if (device.A != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.A);
+            }
+            if (device.B != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.B);
+            }
+            if (device.C != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.C);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            if (device.stream != nullptr) {
+                cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
+            }
         }
+        devices_.clear();
     }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    int dimension_;
+    std::vector<DeviceContext> devices_;
+};
+
+size_t parseMatrixSize(const char* text) {
+    if (text[0] == '\0' || text[0] == '-') {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (*end != '\0' || value == 0 || value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+    if (value > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size exceeds the cuBLAS dimension limit");
+    }
+
+    const size_t N = static_cast<size_t>(value);
+    if (N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / N ||
+        N * N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size is too large");
+    }
+    return N;
 }

    17	// Generate pseudo-random values for matrix initialization
    18	constexpr double getPseudoRndValue(const size_t N, const size_t i, const size_t j) noexcept {
    19	    return (((i + 1) * (i + j + 1) * 1299709) % (N * N)) / static_cast<double>(N * N);
    20	}
    21	
    22	void initMatrix(std::vector<double>& mat, const size_t N) {
    23	    for (size_t i = 0; i < N; ++i) {
    24	        for (size_t j = 0; j < N; ++j) {
    25	            mat[i * N + j] = getPseudoRndValue(N, i, j);
    26	        }
    27	    }
    28	}

    30	const char* cublasStatusString(cublasStatus_t status) noexcept {
    31	    switch (status) {
    32	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS: return "success";
    33	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_INITIALIZED: return "not initialized";
    34	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ALLOC_FAILED: return "allocation failed";
    35	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_INVALID_VALUE: return "invalid value";
    36	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ARCH_MISMATCH: return "architecture mismatch";
    37	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_MAPPING_ERROR: return "mapping error";
    38	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_EXECUTION_FAILED: return "execution failed";
    39	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_INTERNAL_ERROR: return "internal error";
    40	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED: return "not supported";
    41	        case CUBLAS_STATUS_LICENSE_ERROR: return "license error";
    42	        default: return "unknown error";
    43	    }
    44	}

    46	void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
    47	    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
    48	        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
    49	    }
    50	}

    52	void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation) {
    53	    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
    54	        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cublasStatusString(status));
    55	    }
    56	}

    58	// Each GPU owns a disjoint group of output rows. B is replicated, while A and
    59	// C are partitioned, so the GEMMs can execute independently and concurrently.
    60	class CudaMatrixMultiplier {
    61	public:
    62	    CudaMatrixMultiplier(const std::vector<double>& A,
    63	                         const std::vector<double>& B,
    64	                         size_t N)
    65	        : N_(N), dimension_(static_cast<int>(N)) {
    66	        int availableDevices = 0;
    67	        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&availableDevices), "cudaGetDeviceCount");
    68	        if (availableDevices == 0) {
    69	            throw std::runtime_error("no CUDA-capable GPU is available");
    70	        }
    71	
    72	        // A slice of at least 256 rows gives cuBLAS enough work to amortize a
    73	        // launch on current GPUs while still scaling larger matrices broadly.
    74	        constexpr size_t targetRowsPerDevice = 256;
    75	        const size_t usefulDevices = std::max<size_t>(1, N_ / targetRowsPerDevice);
    76	        const size_t deviceCount = std::min<size_t>(availableDevices,
    77	                                                    std::min(N_, usefulDevices));
    78	        devices_.reserve(deviceCount);
    79	
    80	        const size_t baseRows = N_ / deviceCount;
    81	        const size_t remainder = N_ % deviceCount;
    82	        size_t firstRow = 0;
    83	
    84	        try {
    85	            for (size_t index = 0; index < deviceCount; ++index) {
    86	                DeviceContext context;
    87	                context.device = static_cast<int>(index);
    88	                context.firstRow = firstRow;
    89	                context.rows = baseRows + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0);
    90	                devices_.push_back(context);
    91	
    92	                DeviceContext& device = devices_.back();
    93	                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
    94	                checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&device.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
    95	                          "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
    96	                checkCublas(cublasCreate(&device.handle), "cublasCreate");
    97	                checkCublas(cublasSetStream(device.handle, device.stream), "cublasSetStream");
    98	
    99	                const size_t sliceElements = device.rows * N_;
   100	                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.A),
   101	                                     sliceElements * sizeof(double)),
   102	                          "cudaMalloc(A slice)");
   103	                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.B),
   104	                                     N_ * N_ * sizeof(double)),
   105	                          "cudaMalloc(B)");
   106	                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.C),
   107	                                     sliceElements * sizeof(double)),
   108	                          "cudaMalloc(C slice)");
   109	
   110	                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.A, A.data() + firstRow * N_,
   111	                                          sliceElements * sizeof(double),
   112	                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream),
   113	                          "cudaMemcpyAsync(A slice)");
   114	                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.B, B.data(), N_ * N_ * sizeof(double),
   115	                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream),
   116	                          "cudaMemcpyAsync(B)");
   117	
   118	                firstRow += device.rows;
   119	            }
   120	            synchronize();
   121	
   122	            // Warm up CUDA context/module loading so it is not charged to the
   123	            // benchmark. The measured GEMM completely overwrites this result.
   124	            launchGemm();
   125	            synchronize();
   126	        } catch (...) {
   127	            release();
   128	            throw;
   129	        }
   130	    }
   131	
   132	    CudaMatrixMultiplier(const CudaMatrixMultiplier&) = delete;
   133	    CudaMatrixMultiplier& operator=(const CudaMatrixMultiplier&) = delete;
   134	
   135	    ~CudaMatrixMultiplier() {
   136	        release();
   137	    }
   138	
   139	    double multiply() {
   140	        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
   141	        launchGemm();
   142	        synchronize();
   143	        const auto end = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
   144	        return std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(end - start).count();
   145	    }
   146	
   147	    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
   148	        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
   149	            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
   150	            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * N_, device.C,
   151	                                      device.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
   152	                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, device.stream),
   153	                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C slice)");
   154	        }
   155	        synchronize();
   156	    }
   157	
   158	    size_t deviceCount() const noexcept {
   159	        return devices_.size();
   160	    }
   161	
   162	private:
   163	    struct DeviceContext {
   164	        int device = 0;
   165	        size_t firstRow = 0;
   166	        size_t rows = 0;
   167	        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
   168	        cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
   169	        double* A = nullptr;
   170	        double* B = nullptr;
   171	        double* C = nullptr;
   172	    };
   173	
   174	    void launchGemm() {
   175	        constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
   176	        constexpr double beta = 0.0;
   177	        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
   178	            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
   179	
   180	            // cuBLAS uses column-major storage. A row-major C = A * B is the
   181	            // same memory as column-major C^T = B^T * A^T, hence the swapped
   182	            // operand order below. A partition becomes N-by-rows in this view.
   183	            checkCublas(cublasDgemm(device.handle,
   184	                                    CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
   185	                                    dimension_, static_cast<int>(device.rows), dimension_,
   186	                                    &alpha,
   187	                                    device.B, dimension_,
   188	                                    device.A, dimension_,
   189	                                    &beta,
   190	                                    device.C, dimension_),
   191	                        "cublasDgemm");
   192	        }
   193	    }
   194	
   195	    void synchronize() {
   196	        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
   197	            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
   198	            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
   199	        }
   200	    }
   201	
   202	    void release() noexcept {
   203	        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
   204	            cudaSetDevice(device.device);
   205	            if (device.handle != nullptr) {
   206	                cublasDestroy(device.handle);
   207	            }
   208	            if (device.A != nullptr) {
   209	                cudaFree(device.A);
   210	            }
   211	            if (device.B != nullptr) {
   212	                cudaFree(device.B);
   213	            }
   214	            if (device.C != nullptr) {
   215	                cudaFree(device.C);
   216	            }
   217	            if (device.stream != nullptr) {
   218	                cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
   219	            }
   220	        }
   221	        devices_.clear();
   222	    }
   223	
   224	    size_t N_;
   225	    int dimension_;
   226	    std::vector<DeviceContext> devices_;
   227	};
   228	
   229	size_t parseMatrixSize(const char* text) {
   230	    if (text[0] == '\0' || text[0] == '-') {
   231	        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
   232	    }
   233	
   234	    char* end = nullptr;
   235	    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
   236	    if (*end != '\0' || value == 0 || value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
   237	        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
   238	    }
   239	    if (value > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
   240	        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size exceeds the cuBLAS dimension limit");
   241	    }
   242	
   243	    const size_t N = static_cast<size_t>(value);
   244	    if (N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / N ||
   245	        N * N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
   246	        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size is too large");
   247	    }
   248	    return N;
   249	}
   250	
   251	// Simple validation: compute a single element and compare
   252	bool validateResult(const std::vector<double>& A, const std::vector<double>& B,
   253	                   const std::vector<double>& C, const size_t N) {
   254	    // Include the beginning, partition interiors, and the last row/column so
   255	    // multi-GPU slice boundaries are covered as well as the first slice.
   256	    const size_t checkPoints[] = {0, 1 % N, N / 4, N / 2, N - 1};
   257	    
   258	    for (size_t pi = 0; pi < 5; ++pi) {
   259	        for (size_t pj = 0; pj < 5; ++pj) {
   260	            const size_t i = checkPoints[pi] % N;
   261	            const size_t j = checkPoints[pj] % N;
   262	            
   263	            double expected = 0.0;
   264	            for (size_t k = 0; k < N; ++k) {
   265	                expected += A[i * N + k] * B[k * N + j];
   266	            }
   267	            
   268	            const double actual = C[i * N + j];
   269	            const double relError = std::abs((actual - expected) / (expected + 1e-10));
   270	            
   271	            if (relError > 1e-6) {
   272	                printf("Validation failed at (%zu, %zu): expected %.10f, got %.10f (error: %.10e)\n",
   273	                       i, j, expected, actual, relError);
   274	                return false;
   275	            }
   276	        }
   277	    }
   278	    
   279	    return true;
   280	}

   286	    printf("  -v           Enable validation\n");
   287	    printf("  -r           Print results for external validation\n");
   288	    printf("  -h           Show this help message\n");
   289	}

+const char* cublasStatusString(cublasStatus_t status) noexcept {
+    switch (status) {
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS: return "success";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_INITIALIZED: return "not initialized";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ALLOC_FAILED: return "allocation failed";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_INVALID_VALUE: return "invalid value";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ARCH_MISMATCH: return "architecture mismatch";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_MAPPING_ERROR: return "mapping error";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_EXECUTION_FAILED: return "execution failed";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_INTERNAL_ERROR: return "internal error";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED: return "not supported";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_LICENSE_ERROR: return "license error";
+        default: return "unknown error";
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCuda(cudaError_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != cudaSuccess) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cudaGetErrorString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+void checkCublas(cublasStatus_t status, const char* operation) {
+    if (status != CUBLAS_STATUS_SUCCESS) {
+        throw std::runtime_error(std::string(operation) + ": " + cublasStatusString(status));
+    }
+}
+
+// Each GPU owns a disjoint group of output rows. B is replicated, while A and
+// C are partitioned, so the GEMMs can execute independently and concurrently.
+class CudaMatrixMultiplier {
+public:
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier(const std::vector<double>& A,
+                         const std::vector<double>& B,
+                         size_t N)
+        : N_(N), dimension_(static_cast<int>(N)) {
+        int availableDevices = 0;
+        checkCuda(cudaGetDeviceCount(&availableDevices), "cudaGetDeviceCount");
+        if (availableDevices == 0) {
+            throw std::runtime_error("no CUDA-capable GPU is available");
+        }
+
+        // A slice of at least 256 rows gives cuBLAS enough work to amortize a
+        // launch on current GPUs while still scaling larger matrices broadly.
+        constexpr size_t targetRowsPerDevice = 256;
+        const size_t usefulDevices = std::max<size_t>(1, N_ / targetRowsPerDevice);
+        const size_t deviceCount = std::min<size_t>(availableDevices,
+                                                    std::min(N_, usefulDevices));
+        devices_.reserve(deviceCount);
+
+        const size_t baseRows = N_ / deviceCount;
+        const size_t remainder = N_ % deviceCount;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+
+        try {
+            for (size_t index = 0; index < deviceCount; ++index) {
+                DeviceContext context;
+                context.device = static_cast<int>(index);
+                context.firstRow = firstRow;
+                context.rows = baseRows + (index < remainder ? 1 : 0);
+                devices_.push_back(context);
+
+                DeviceContext& device = devices_.back();
+                checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+                checkCuda(cudaStreamCreateWithFlags(&device.stream, cudaStreamNonBlocking),
+                          "cudaStreamCreateWithFlags");
+                checkCublas(cublasCreate(&device.handle), "cublasCreate");
+                checkCublas(cublasSetStream(device.handle, device.stream), "cublasSetStream");
+
+                const size_t sliceElements = device.rows * N_;
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.A),
+                                     sliceElements * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(A slice)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.B),
+                                     N_ * N_ * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(B)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMalloc(reinterpret_cast<void**>(&device.C),
+                                     sliceElements * sizeof(double)),
+                          "cudaMalloc(C slice)");
+
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.A, A.data() + firstRow * N_,
+                                          sliceElements * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync(A slice)");
+                checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(device.B, B.data(), N_ * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                          cudaMemcpyHostToDevice, device.stream),
+                          "cudaMemcpyAsync(B)");
+
+                firstRow += device.rows;
+            }
+            synchronize();
+
+            // Warm up CUDA context/module loading so it is not charged to the
+            // benchmark. The measured GEMM completely overwrites this result.
+            launchGemm();
+            synchronize();
+        } catch (...) {
+            release();
+            throw;
+        }
+    }
+
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier(const CudaMatrixMultiplier&) = delete;
+    CudaMatrixMultiplier& operator=(const CudaMatrixMultiplier&) = delete;
+
+    ~CudaMatrixMultiplier() {
+        release();
+    }
+
+    double multiply() {
+        const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+        launchGemm();
+        synchronize();
+        const auto end = std::chrono::steady_clock::now();
+        return std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(end - start).count();
+    }
+
+    void copyResult(std::vector<double>& C) {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * N_, device.C,
+                                      device.rows * N_ * sizeof(double),
+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, device.stream),
+                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C slice)");
+        }
+        synchronize();
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceCount() const noexcept {
+        return devices_.size();
+    }
+
+private:
+    struct DeviceContext {
+        int device = 0;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+        size_t rows = 0;
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+        double* A = nullptr;
+        double* B = nullptr;
+        double* C = nullptr;
+    };
+
+    void launchGemm() {
+        constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+        constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+
+            // cuBLAS uses column-major storage. A row-major C = A * B is the
+            // same memory as column-major C^T = B^T * A^T, hence the swapped
+            // operand order below. A partition becomes N-by-rows in this view.
+            checkCublas(cublasDgemm(device.handle,
+                                    CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                    dimension_, static_cast<int>(device.rows), dimension_,
+                                    &alpha,
+                                    device.B, dimension_,
+                                    device.A, dimension_,
+                                    &beta,
+                                    device.C, dimension_),
+                        "cublasDgemm");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() noexcept {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            cudaSetDevice(device.device);
+            if (device.handle != nullptr) {
+                cublasDestroy(device.handle);
+            }
+            if (device.A != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.A);
+            }
+            if (device.B != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.B);
+            }
+            if (device.C != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.C);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            if (device.stream != nullptr) {
+                cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
+            }
         }
+        devices_.clear();
     }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    int dimension_;
+    std::vector<DeviceContext> devices_;
+};
+
+size_t parseMatrixSize(const char* text) {
+    if (text[0] == '\0' || text[0] == '-') {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (*end != '\0' || value == 0 || value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+    if (value > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size exceeds the cuBLAS dimension limit");
+    }
+
+    const size_t N = static_cast<size_t>(value);
+    if (N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / N ||
+        N * N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size is too large");
+    }
+    return N;
 }

+            checkCuda(cudaMemcpyAsync(C.data() + device.firstRow * N_, device.C,

+                                      cudaMemcpyDeviceToHost, device.stream),
+                      "cudaMemcpyAsync(C slice)");
+        }
+        synchronize();
+    }
+
+    size_t deviceCount() const noexcept {
+        return devices_.size();
+    }
+
+private:
+    struct DeviceContext {
+        int device = 0;
+        size_t firstRow = 0;
+        size_t rows = 0;
+        cudaStream_t stream = nullptr;
+        cublasHandle_t handle = nullptr;
+        double* A = nullptr;
+        double* B = nullptr;
+        double* C = nullptr;
+    };
+
+    void launchGemm() {
+        constexpr double alpha = 1.0;
+        constexpr double beta = 0.0;
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+
+            // cuBLAS uses column-major storage. A row-major C = A * B is the
+            // same memory as column-major C^T = B^T * A^T, hence the swapped
+            // operand order below. A partition becomes N-by-rows in this view.
+            checkCublas(cublasDgemm(device.handle,
+                                    CUBLAS_OP_N, CUBLAS_OP_N,
+                                    dimension_, static_cast<int>(device.rows), dimension_,
+                                    &alpha,
+                                    device.B, dimension_,
+                                    device.A, dimension_,
+                                    &beta,
+                                    device.C, dimension_),
+                        "cublasDgemm");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void synchronize() {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            checkCuda(cudaSetDevice(device.device), "cudaSetDevice");
+            checkCuda(cudaStreamSynchronize(device.stream), "cudaStreamSynchronize");
+        }
+    }
+
+    void release() noexcept {
+        for (DeviceContext& device : devices_) {
+            cudaSetDevice(device.device);
+            if (device.handle != nullptr) {
+                cublasDestroy(device.handle);
+            }
+            if (device.A != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.A);
+            }
+            if (device.B != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.B);
+            }
+            if (device.C != nullptr) {
+                cudaFree(device.C);
             }
-            C[i * N + j] = sum;
+            if (device.stream != nullptr) {
+                cudaStreamDestroy(device.stream);
+            }
         }
+        devices_.clear();
     }
+
+    size_t N_;
+    int dimension_;
+    std::vector<DeviceContext> devices_;
+};
+
+size_t parseMatrixSize(const char* text) {
+    if (text[0] == '\0' || text[0] == '-') {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+
+    char* end = nullptr;
+    const unsigned long long value = std::strtoull(text, &end, 10);
+    if (*end != '\0' || value == 0 || value > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max()) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size must be a positive integer");
+    }
+    if (value > static_cast<unsigned long long>(std::numeric_limits<int>::max())) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size exceeds the cuBLAS dimension limit");
+    }
+
+    const size_t N = static_cast<size_t>(value);
+    if (N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / N ||
+        N * N > std::numeric_limits<size_t>::max() / sizeof(double)) {
+        throw std::invalid_argument("matrix size is too large");
+    }
+    return N;
 }

+    switch (status) {

+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_NOT_INITIALIZED: return "not initialized";
+        case CUBLAS_STATUS_ALLOC_FAILED: return "allocation failed";
